<style>
:root{
  --bg:#F5F6F8;
  --card:#FFFFFF;
  --border:#E1E4E8;
  --text:#252423;
  --text-soft:#605E5C;
  --blue:#118DFF;
  --blue-dark:#0F6CBD;
  --amber:#FFB900;
  --red:#D13438;
  --green:#107C10;
  --shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.06),0 1px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.04);
}
*{box-sizing:border-box;}
body{
  margin:0;
  font-family:"Segoe UI",-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,Roboto,Arial,sans-serif;
  background:var(--bg);
  color:var(--text);
}
.topbar{
  background:var(--card);
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);
  padding:14px 24px;
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:space-between;
  flex-wrap:wrap;
  gap:12px;
  position:sticky;
  top:0;
  z-index:20;
}
.topbar h1{
  font-size:18px;
  margin:0;
  font-weight:600;
}
.topbar .sub{
  font-size:12px;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  margin-top:2px;
}
.controls{
  display:flex;
  gap:10px;
  align-items:center;
  flex-wrap:wrap;
}
.controls label{
  font-size:12px;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  margin-right:4px;
}
input[type=date], input[type=text], select{
  font-family:inherit;
  font-size:13px;
  padding:6px 10px;
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius:4px;
  background:#fff;
  color:var(--text);
}
button{
  font-family:inherit;
  font-size:13px;
  padding:7px 14px;
  border-radius:4px;
  border:1px solid var(--blue);
  background:var(--blue);
  color:#fff;
  cursor:pointer;
  font-weight:600;
}
button.secondary{
  background:#fff;
  color:var(--blue);
}
button.ghost{
  background:#fff;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  border-color:var(--border);
  font-weight:400;
}
button:hover{ filter:brightness(0.95); }
#fileInput{ display:none; }
#billingFileInput{ display:none; }

.wrap{ padding:20px 24px 60px; max-width:1400px; margin:0 auto; }

.kpis{
  display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(190px,1fr));
  gap:12px;
  margin-bottom:20px;
}
.kpi{
  background:var(--card);
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius:6px;
  padding:14px 16px;
  box-shadow:var(--shadow);
}
.kpi .val{ font-size:26px; font-weight:700; line-height:1.1;}
.kpi .lbl{ font-size:12px; color:var(--text-soft); margin-top:4px;}
.kpi.blue .val{ color:var(--blue-dark); }
.kpi.red .val{ color:var(--red); }
.kpi.green .val{ color:var(--green); }
.kpi.amber .val{ color:#B36B00; }

.panel{
  background:var(--card);
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius:6px;
  box-shadow:var(--shadow);
  margin-bottom:20px;
  overflow:hidden;
}
.panel-head{
  padding:12px 16px;
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:space-between;
  flex-wrap:wrap;
  gap:8px;
}
.panel-head h2{ font-size:14px; margin:0; font-weight:600; }
.panel-body{ padding:14px 16px; }

table{ width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; font-size:13px; }
th{
  text-align:left;
  padding:8px 10px;
  border-bottom:2px solid var(--border);
  color:var(--text-soft);
  font-weight:600;
  font-size:12px;
  text-transform:uppercase;
  letter-spacing:.02em;
  cursor:pointer;
  white-space:nowrap;
}
th:hover{ color:var(--blue-dark); }
td{
  padding:8px 10px;
  border-bottom:1px solid #F0F0F0;
  white-space:nowrap;
}
tr:hover td{ background:#FAFBFC; }
.olt-row{ cursor:pointer; font-weight:600; }
.olt-row td{ background:#F8FAFD; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border); }
.olt-row:hover td{ background:#EEF5FF; }
.bar-cell{ display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; min-width:140px; }
.bar-track{ flex:1; height:8px; background:#EDEDED; border-radius:4px; overflow:hidden; min-width:60px; }
.bar-fill{ height:100%; background:var(--blue); border-radius:4px; }
.pill{
  display:inline-block;
  padding:2px 8px;
  border-radius:10px;
  font-size:11px;
  font-weight:600;
}
.pill.counter{ background:#FFF4CE; color:#8A6D00; }
.pill.online{ background:#DFF6DD; color:#107C10; }
.pill.na{ background:#F0F0F0; color:#8A8886; }
.followBtn{
  font-size:11px;
  padding:4px 10px;
  border-radius:12px;
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  background:#fff;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  font-weight:600;
  cursor:pointer;
  white-space:nowrap;
}
.followBtn:hover{ border-color:var(--blue); color:var(--blue-dark); }
.followBtn.done{
  background:#DFF6DD;
  color:var(--green);
  border-color:#B3E6B3;
}
.remarksInput{
  font-size:12px;
  padding:5px 8px;
  border:1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius:4px;
  width:160px;
}

.customer-block{ display:none; }
.customer-block.open{ display:block; }
.customer-block .panel-body{ background:#FCFCFD; }

.note{
  font-size:12px;
  color:var(--text-soft);
  background:#FFF9E6;
  border:1px solid #FFE9A8;
  border-radius:4px;
  padding:8px 12px;
  margin-bottom:12px;
}
.empty{ text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--text-soft); font-size:13px; }
.footer-row{ display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-top:10px; font-size:12px; color:var(--text-soft); }
.tag-source{ font-size:10px; padding:1px 6px; border-radius:8px; background:#EEF5FF; color:var(--blue-dark); margin-left:6px; }
::-webkit-scrollbar{ height:8px; width:8px; }
::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{ background:#D7D7D7; border-radius:4px; }
.scrollx{ overflow-x:auto; }

/* ---- Login overlay ---- */
#loginOverlay{
  position:fixed; inset:0; background:rgba(20,20,25,0.55); display:flex;
  align-items:center; justify-content:center; z-index:9999;
}
#loginBox{
  background:var(--card); border-radius:12px; padding:28px 32px; width:320px;
  box-shadow:0 8px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.25);
}
#loginBox h2{ margin:0 0 4px 0; font-size:18px; }
#loginBox p{ margin:0 0 16px 0; font-size:12px; color:var(--text-soft); }
#loginBox label{ font-size:12px; color:var(--text-soft); display:block; margin-bottom:4px; }
#loginBox input{ width:100%; padding:8px 10px; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:6px; margin-bottom:12px; font-size:14px; }
#loginBox button{ width:100%; padding:9px; border:none; border-radius:6px; background:var(--blue); color:#fff; font-weight:600; cursor:pointer; }
#loginBox button:hover{ background:var(--blue-dark); }
#loginErr{ color:var(--red); font-size:12px; margin-top:-6px; margin-bottom:10px; min-height:14px; }
#staffBadge{ font-size:12px; background:#EEF6FF; color:var(--blue-dark); padding:5px 10px; border-radius:14px; font-weight:600; display:inline-flex; align-items:center; gap:6px; }
#staffBadge button{ border:none; background:transparent; color:var(--red); cursor:pointer; font-size:11px; font-weight:600; }

/* ---- High risk panel ---- */
.hr-badge{ background:#FDE7E9; color:var(--red); border:1px solid #F3B7BB; border-radius:10px; padding:1px 6px; font-size:10px; font-weight:700; margin-left:6px; }
.risk-hi{ color:var(--red); font-weight:700; }
.risk-med{ color:#C97A00; font-weight:700; }
#highRiskPanel .panel-head{ background:#FFF4F4; }
#highRiskPanel h2{ color:var(--red); }

/* ---- Remarks dropdown ---- */
select.remarksInput{
  padding:5px 6px; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:6px; font-size:12px; max-width:190px;
}

/* ---- Summary report panel ---- */
#summaryPanel table{ font-size:12px; }
#summaryPanel td, #summaryPanel th{ padding:6px 8px; }
.cat-pill{ display:inline-block; background:#F3F2F1; border-radius:10px; padding:1px 7px; font-size:10px; margin:1px 2px; color:var(--text-soft); }

/* ---- Top-level section tabs ---- */
.mainTabs{
  display:flex; gap:4px; padding:0 24px; background:var(--card);
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--border); position:sticky; top:64px; z-index:19; overflow-x:auto;
}
.mainTab{
  background:transparent; border:none; border-bottom:3px solid transparent; border-radius:0;
  color:var(--text-soft); font-weight:600; font-size:13px; padding:12px 16px; cursor:pointer; white-space:nowrap;
}
.mainTab:hover{ color:var(--blue-dark); filter:none; }
.mainTab.active{ color:var(--blue-dark); border-bottom-color:var(--blue); }
.tabSection{ display:none; }
.tabSection.active{ display:block; }

/* ---- Customer list modal ---- */
#custModalOverlay{
  position:fixed; inset:0; background:rgba(20,20,25,0.55); display:none;
  align-items:flex-start; justify-content:center; z-index:9998; padding:40px 20px; overflow-y:auto;
}
#custModalOverlay.open{ display:flex; }
#custModalBox{
  background:var(--card); border-radius:8px; width:100%; max-width:1100px;
  box-shadow:0 8px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); max-height:calc(100vh - 80px); display:flex; flex-direction:column;
}
#custModalBox .panel-head{ flex:none; }
#custModalBox .panel-body{ overflow-y:auto; }
#custModalClose{
  background:transparent; border:none; color:var(--text-soft); font-size:20px; font-weight:400;
  cursor:pointer; padding:2px 8px; line-height:1;
}
#custModalClose:hover{ color:var(--red); filter:none; }
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div id="loginOverlay">
  <div id="loginBox">
    <h2>Staff Login</h2>
    <p>Expiry Tracker access garna staff ID ra password halnus.</p>
    <label for="loginUser">Staff ID</label>
    <input type="text" id="loginUser" autocomplete="username" placeholder="e.g. luksha">
    <label for="loginPass">Password</label>
    <input type="password" id="loginPass" autocomplete="current-password" placeholder="••••••••">
    <div id="loginErr"></div>
    <button id="loginBtn">Login</button>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="topbar">
  <div>
    <h1>Expiry Tracker — Shuklagandaki (Region 5)</h1>
    <div class="sub" id="dataMeta">Loading…</div>
  </div>
  <div class="controls">
    <div>
      <label for="uptoDate">Expire भएका (up to)</label>
      <input type="date" id="uptoDate">
    </div>
    <div>
      <label for="oltFilter">OLT</label>
      <select id="oltFilter"><option value="ALL">All OLTs</option></select>
    </div>
    <button class="secondary" id="importBtn">Excel Import</button>
    <input type="file" id="fileInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv">
    <button class="secondary" id="billingImportBtn">Billing Import</button>
    <input type="file" id="billingFileInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv">
    <button class="ghost" id="exportAllBtn">Export CSV</button>
    <span id="staffBadge" style="display:none;"></span>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="mainTabs">
  <button class="mainTab active" data-tab="tabOverview">Overview</button>
  <button class="mainTab" data-tab="tabExpiry">Expiry Users</button>
  <button class="mainTab" data-tab="tabHighRisk">High Risk Users</button>
  <button class="mainTab" data-tab="tabSummary">Summary Reports</button>
</div>

<div class="wrap">

  <div class="note" id="paymentNote">
    Yo dataset ma <b>Counter / Online payment</b> field xaina — jun file ma <i>Payment Mode / Payment Type / Channel</i> jasto column huncha, tyo import garepachi automatic detect भएर breakdown देखिन्छ।
  </div>

  <!-- ============ OVERVIEW TAB ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection active" id="tabOverview">
    <div class="kpis" id="kpiRow"></div>
    <div class="note" id="billingNote" style="display:none;"></div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ EXPIRY USERS TAB ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection" id="tabExpiry">
    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>OLT-wise Expired Users (till selected date)</h2>
        <span id="oltPanelSub" class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="scrollx">
        <table id="oltTable">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th data-k="olt">OLT</th>
              <th data-k="count">Total User</th>
              <th data-k="followup">Total Follow-up</th>
              <th data-k="pending">Bill Pending (NPR)</th>
              <th data-k="revenue">Revenue at Stake (NPR)</th>
              <th data-k="counter">Counter</th>
              <th data-k="online">Online</th>
              <th data-k="share">Share</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="oltTbody"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
        <div class="footer-row"><span>OLT row click garera tyo OLT ko customer list modal ma pani herna sakincha.</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>All Expiring Users</h2>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Search username/plan..." class="searchBox" id="expiryListSearch" style="width:200px;">
          <span id="expiryListSub" class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);"></span>
          <button class="ghost" id="expiryListExport">Export CSV</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="scrollx">
        <table>
          <thead><tr>
            <th>Username</th><th>OLT</th><th>Follow-up</th><th>Remarks</th><th>Expiry Date</th><th>Plan</th><th>Bandwidth</th><th>Revenue</th><th>Payment</th>
          </tr></thead>
          <tbody id="expiryListBody"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ HIGH RISK USERS TAB ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection" id="tabHighRisk">
    <div class="panel" id="highRiskPanel">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>🔴 High Risk — Not Renewed (Habitual Late Payers)</h2>
        <span class="sub" id="highRiskSub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div class="scrollx">
        <table>
          <thead><tr>
            <th>Username</th><th>OLT</th><th>Risk Score</th><th>Type</th><th>Status</th><th>Fore. Revenue</th><th>Follow-up</th><th>Remarks</th>
          </tr></thead>
          <tbody id="highRiskBody"></tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ SUMMARY REPORTS TAB ============ -->
  <div class="tabSection" id="tabSummary">
    <div class="panel" id="summaryPanelWrap">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <h2>📊 Staff-wise Summary Report</h2>
        <span class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-soft);">Follow-ups, effort hours, ra collected billing staff anusar</span>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body" id="summaryPanel"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

</div>

<!-- ============ Customer list modal (opens on OLT row click) ============ -->
<div id="custModalOverlay">
  <div id="custModalBox">
    <div class="panel-head">
      <h2 id="custModalTitle">Customer List</h2>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;">
        <input type="text" placeholder="Search username/plan..." class="searchBox" id="custModalSearch" style="width:200px;">
        <button class="ghost" id="custModalExport">Export CSV</button>
        <button id="custModalClose">&times;</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel-body">
      <div class="scrollx">
      <table>
        <thead><tr>
          <th>Username</th><th>Follow-up</th><th>Remarks</th><th>Expiry Date</th><th>Plan</th><th>Bandwidth</th><th>Revenue</th><th>Payment</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody id="custModalBody"></tbody>
      </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
let STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL = {}; // id -> minutes (today, from Firebase) — declared early to avoid TDZ issues
let RAW = [];        // active dataset (array of row objects, normalized keys)
let COLMETA = { hasPayment:false, paymentKey:null };
let BILLING_PAID = new Set();   // normalized usernames found in imported billing data
let BILLING_AMOUNT = {};        // normalized username -> paid amount (if billing file has an amount column)
let BILLING_META = { fileName:'', count:0 };
let HR_FOLLOWUPS = {};          // normalized username -> {username, followUp, remarks, followedUpBy, followedUpByName} for High-Risk list

function normUser(u){ return String(u||'').trim().toLowerCase(); }

// ---------- OLT extraction ----------
function deriveOLT(username){
  if(!username) return 'UNMAPPED';
  const u = String(username).toLowerCase();
  const suffix = u.includes('_') ? u.split('_').pop() : u;
  if(/skgd|skhd|skga|sskgd/.test(suffix)) return 'SKGD01';
  if(/vmad|vmd|vamd|vmqd/.test(suffix))   return 'VMAD01';
  if(/rish/.test(suffix))                 return 'RISH01';
  return 'OTHER (' + suffix.toUpperCase() + ')';
}

// ---------- normalize a raw imported row into our schema ----------
function findKey(row, patterns){
  const keys = Object.keys(row);
  for(const k of keys){
    const lk = k.toLowerCase();
    for(const p of patterns){
      if(lk.includes(p)) return k;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function normalizeDataset(rows){
  if(!rows.length) return [];
  const sample = rows[0];
  const userKey = findKey(sample, ['username']) || Object.keys(sample)[0];
  const expiryKey = findKey(sample, ['expiry']);
  const revenueKey = findKey(sample, ['revenue']);
  const planKey = findKey(sample, ['plan name','planname','plan']);
  const bandwidthKey = findKey(sample, ['bandwidth']);
  const statusKey = findKey(sample, ['transaction date','status','renew']);
  const paymentKey = findKey(sample, ['payment mode','payment type','payment channel','payment','channel']);

  COLMETA.hasPayment = !!paymentKey;
  COLMETA.paymentKey = paymentKey;

  return rows.map(r=>{
    let expiry = r[expiryKey];
    if(expiry instanceof Date){
      expiry = expiry.toISOString().slice(0,10);
    } else if(typeof expiry === 'number'){
      // excel serial date
      const d = XLSX.SSF.parse_date_code(expiry);
      expiry = d ? `${d.y}-${String(d.m).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d.d).padStart(2,'0')}` : '';
    } else if(typeof expiry === 'string' && expiry.length>10){
      expiry = expiry.slice(0,10);
    }
    return {
      username: r[userKey] || '',
      olt: deriveOLT(r[userKey]),
      expiry: expiry || '',
      status: statusKey ? r[statusKey] : '',
      revenue: revenueKey ? (parseFloat(r[revenueKey]) || 0) : 0,
      plan: planKey ? r[planKey] : '',
      bandwidth: bandwidthKey ? r[bandwidthKey] : '',
      payment: paymentKey ? (r[paymentKey] || 'N/A') : 'N/A'
    };
  });
}

function classifyPayment(val){
  const v = String(val || '').toLowerCase();
  if(v.includes('online') || v.includes('esewa') || v.includes('khalti') || v.includes('bank') || v.includes('card')) return 'online';
  if(v.includes('counter') || v.includes('cash')) return 'counter';
  return null;
}

// ---------- rendering ----------
function fmtNum(n){ return Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-IN'); }

function getUptoDate(){
  return document.getElementById('uptoDate').value;
}

function filteredRows(){
  const upto = getUptoDate();
  const oltSel = document.getElementById('oltFilter').value;
  return RAW.filter(r=>{
    if(!r.expiry) return false;
    if(upto && r.expiry > upto) return false;
    if(oltSel !== 'ALL' && r.olt !== oltSel) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function render(){
  const rows = filteredRows();

  // KPIs
  const totalUsers = rows.length;
  const totalRevenue = rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.revenue,0);
  const oltCount = new Set(rows.map(r=>r.olt)).size;
  let counterN=0, onlineN=0, naN=0;
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    const c = classifyPayment(r.payment);
    if(c==='counter') counterN++; else if(c==='online') onlineN++; else naN++;
  });

  const followUpTotal = rows.reduce((s,r)=> s + (r.followUp ? 1 : 0), 0);
  const paymentDoneTotal = BILLING_PAID.size ? rows.reduce((s,r)=> s + (BILLING_PAID.has(normUser(r.username)) ? 1 : 0), 0) : 0;

  const kpiRow = document.getElementById('kpiRow');
  kpiRow.innerHTML = `
    <div class="kpi red"><div class="val">${fmtNum(totalUsers)}</div><div class="lbl">Total Expired Users</div></div>
    <div class="kpi blue"><div class="val">${fmtNum(totalRevenue)}</div><div class="lbl">Revenue at Stake (NPR)</div></div>
    <div class="kpi amber"><div class="val">${oltCount}</div><div class="lbl">OLTs Affected</div></div>
    <div class="kpi green"><div class="val">${COLMETA.hasPayment ? (counterN+'/'+onlineN) : '—'}</div><div class="lbl">Counter / Online split</div></div>
    <div class="kpi amber"><div class="val">${fmtNum(followUpTotal)}</div><div class="lbl">Follow-up Total</div></div>
    <div class="kpi green"><div class="val">${BILLING_PAID.size ? fmtNum(paymentDoneTotal) : '—'}</div><div class="lbl">Payment Done</div></div>
  `;

  document.getElementById('paymentNote').style.display = COLMETA.hasPayment ? 'none' : 'block';

  const billingNote = document.getElementById('billingNote');
  billingNote.style.display = 'block';
  if(BILLING_PAID.size){
    billingNote.textContent = `Billing data loaded: ${BILLING_META.fileName} · ${fmtNum(BILLING_META.count)} unique users. "Payment Done" upar tyahi bata calculate bhako ho.`;
  } else {
    billingNote.textContent = 'Billing data import gareko xaina — "Billing Import" button bata renewal/payment file (jasto USERNAME + RECENT INVOICE RENEW DATE column bhayeko) upload garepachi "Payment Done" count dekhincha.';
  }

  // OLT grouping
  const groups = {};
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    if(!groups[r.olt]) groups[r.olt] = {olt:r.olt, count:0, revenue:0, counter:0, online:0, na:0, followUp:0, pending:0, rows:[]};
    const g = groups[r.olt];
    g.count++; g.revenue += r.revenue; g.rows.push(r);
    const c = classifyPayment(r.payment);
    if(c==='counter') g.counter++; else if(c==='online') g.online++; else g.na++;
    if(r.followUp) g.followUp++;
    if(!BILLING_PAID.has(normUser(r.username))) g.pending += r.revenue;
  });
  const groupArr = Object.values(groups).sort((a,b)=>b.count-a.count);
  const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...groupArr.map(g=>g.count));

  const tbody = document.getElementById('oltTbody');
  tbody.innerHTML = '';

  if(groupArr.length===0){
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty">Selected filter/date ma expired user chaina</td></tr>`;
  }

  groupArr.forEach((g)=>{
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.className = 'olt-row';
    tr.dataset.olt = g.olt;
    const share = ((g.count/totalUsers)*100).toFixed(1);
    tr.innerHTML = `
      <td>👁 ${g.olt}</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(g.count)}</td>
      <td><span class="pill online">${fmtNum(g.followUp)}</span> / ${fmtNum(g.count)}</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(g.pending)}</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(g.revenue)}</td>
      <td>${COLMETA.hasPayment ? `<span class="pill counter">${g.counter}</span>` : `<span class="pill na">N/A</span>`}</td>
      <td>${COLMETA.hasPayment ? `<span class="pill online">${g.online}</span>` : `<span class="pill na">N/A</span>`}</td>
      <td><div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-track"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:${(g.count/maxCount*100).toFixed(0)}%"></div></div><span>${share}%</span></div></td>
    `;
    tr.addEventListener('click', ()=> openCustomerModal(g));
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  });

  document.getElementById('oltPanelSub').textContent = `Upto: ${getUptoDate() || 'all'} · Filter: ${document.getElementById('oltFilter').value}`;

  renderExpiryList(rows);

  // populate OLT filter options (once meaningful)
  const sel = document.getElementById('oltFilter');
  const existing = new Set(Array.from(sel.options).map(o=>o.value));
  const allOlts = Array.from(new Set(RAW.map(r=>r.olt))).sort();
  allOlts.forEach(o=>{
    if(!existing.has(o)){
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = o; opt.textContent = o;
      sel.appendChild(opt);
    }
  });

  if(typeof renderSummary === 'function') renderSummary();
}

let EXPIRY_LIST_ALL = []; // full filtered rows (from render()), before search box narrows it
function renderExpiryList(rows){
  EXPIRY_LIST_ALL = rows;

  const list = q ? rows.filter(r=> (r.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (r.plan||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)) : rows;

    <tr>
      <td>${r.username}</td>
      <td>${r.olt}</td>
      <td><button class="followBtn expListFollowBtn ${r.followUp?'done':''}" data-uname="${r.username}">${r.followUp ? '✓ Followed up' : 'Mark follow-up'}</button>${window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__ && window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__.some(h=>normUser(h.Username)===normUser(r.username)) ? '<span class="hr-badge">HIGH RISK</span>' : ''}</td>

      <td>${r.expiry}</td>

  `).join('') || `<tr><td colspan="9" class="empty">No records</td></tr>`;
}

document.getElementById('expiryListBody').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('expListFollowBtn')){
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); return; }
    const uname = e.target.dataset.uname;
    const rowObj = RAW.find(rr=>rr.username===uname);

      rowObj.followUp = !rowObj.followUp;

      fbSaveFollowup(rowObj.username, rowObj.followUp, rowObj.remarks, 'main');
      renderSummary();

});

    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); e.target.value=''; return; }

    const rowObj = RAW.find(rr=>rr.username===uname);

      rowObj.followedUpByName = CURRENT_STAFF.name;

});

'<span class="hr-badge">HIGH RISK</span>' : ''}</td>

// ---------- Top-level tab switching ----------
document.querySelectorAll('.mainTab').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    document.querySelectorAll('.mainTab').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tabSection').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById(btn.dataset.tab).classList.add('active');
  });
});

function exportCSV(rows, filename){
  const headers = ['Username','Follow-up','Remarks','Expiry Date','Plan','Bandwidth','Revenue','Payment','OLT'];
  const lines = [headers.join(',')];
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    lines.push([r.username, r.followUp ? 'Yes' : 'No', `"${(r.remarks||'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`, r.expiry, `"${(r.plan||'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`, r.bandwidth, r.revenue, r.payment, r.olt].join(','));
  });
  const blob = new Blob([lines.join('\n')], {type:'text/csv'});
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url; a.download = filename; a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

// ---------- Firebase sync (follow-up + remarks) ----------
function fbSaveFollowup(username, followUp, remarks, kind){
  if(!fbdb){ setSyncStatus('Offline mode (Firebase not loaded)', true); return; }
  const basePath = (kind === 'highrisk') ? 'expiryTracker/highRiskFollowups' : FB_PATH;
  try{
    const key = fbKey(username);
    fbdb.ref(basePath + '/' + key).set({
      username: username,
      followUp: !!followUp,
      remarks: remarks || '',
      followedUpBy: CURRENT_STAFF ? CURRENT_STAFF.id : null,
      followedUpByName: CURRENT_STAFF ? CURRENT_STAFF.name : null,
      updatedAt: Date.now()
    }).then(()=> setSyncStatus('Synced ✓')).catch(err=>{
      console.error('Firebase save failed', err);
      setSyncStatus('Sync failed — check Firebase rules', true);
    });
    // also log an immutable activity event for audit trail
    if(CURRENT_STAFF){
      fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/activityLog').push({
        staff: CURRENT_STAFF.id, name: CURRENT_STAFF.name, username, followUp: !!followUp,
        remarks: remarks || '', kind: kind || 'main', ts: Date.now()
      });
    }
  }catch(err){
    console.error('Firebase save error', err);
    setSyncStatus('Sync failed', true);
  }
}

function fbLoadAndMerge(){
  if(!fbdb){ setSyncStatus('Offline mode (Firebase not loaded)', true); return; }
  setSyncStatus('Syncing…');
  try{
    fbdb.ref(FB_PATH).once('value').then(snap=>{
      const data = snap.val() || {};
      const map = {};
      Object.values(data).forEach(v=>{ if(v && v.username) map[v.username] = v; });
      RAW.forEach(r=>{
        const rec = map[r.username];
        if(rec){
          r.followUp = !!rec.followUp; r.remarks = rec.remarks || '';
          r.followedUpBy = rec.followedUpBy || null;
          r.followedUpByName = rec.followedUpByName || null;
        }
      });
      setSyncStatus('Synced with Firebase ✓');
      render();
      renderSummary();
    }).catch(err=>{
      console.error('Firebase load failed', err);
      setSyncStatus('Firebase load failed — offline mode', true);
    });
  }catch(err){
    console.error('Firebase load error', err);
    setSyncStatus('Offline mode', true);
  }
  // load high-risk followups
  try{
    fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/highRiskFollowups').once('value').then(snap=>{
      const data = snap.val() || {};
      HR_FOLLOWUPS = {};
      Object.values(data).forEach(v=>{ if(v && v.username) HR_FOLLOWUPS[normUser(v.username)] = v; });
      renderHighRisk();
      renderSummary();
    }).catch(err=> console.error('High-risk load failed', err));
  }catch(err){ console.error('High-risk load error', err); }
}

function setSyncStatus(msg, isError){
  let el = document.getElementById('syncStatus');
  if(!el){
    el = document.createElement('span');
    el.id = 'syncStatus';
    el.style.marginLeft = '8px';
    el.style.fontSize = '11px';
    document.getElementById('dataMeta').appendChild(el);
  }
  el.textContent = ' · ' + msg;
  el.style.color = isError ? 'var(--red)' : 'var(--green)';
}

// ---------- file import ----------
document.getElementById('importBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=> document.getElementById('fileInput').click());
document.getElementById('fileInput').addEventListener('change', (e)=>{
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (evt)=>{
    const data = new Uint8Array(evt.target.result);
    const wb = XLSX.read(data, {type:'array', cellDates:true});
    const sheet = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
    // find header row: try row 0 first, else scan for a row containing 'Username'
    let json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {defval:''});
    if(json.length && !findKey(json[0], ['username'])){
      // try re-parsing with header detection by scanning rows
      const raw = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {header:1, defval:''});
      let headerRowIdx = raw.findIndex(row => row.some(c => String(c).toLowerCase().includes('username')));
      if(headerRowIdx >= 0){
        json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {range:headerRowIdx, defval:''});
      }
    }
    RAW = normalizeDataset(json);
    document.getElementById('dataMeta').textContent = `Imported: ${file.name} · ${RAW.length} records`;
    setDefaultUptoDate();
    render();
    fbLoadAndMerge();
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
});

document.getElementById('billingImportBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=> document.getElementById('billingFileInput').click());
document.getElementById('billingFileInput').addEventListener('change', (e)=>{
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (ev)=>{
    const data = new Uint8Array(ev.target.result);
    const wb = XLSX.read(data, {type:'array', cellDates:true});
    const sheet = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
    let json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {defval:''});
    if(!json.length || !findKey(json[0], ['username'])){
      const raw = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {header:1, defval:''});
      const headerRowIdx = raw.findIndex(row => row.some(c => String(c).toLowerCase().includes('username')));
      if(headerRowIdx >= 0){
        json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, {range:headerRowIdx, defval:''});
      }
    }
    if(!json.length){
      BILLING_META = { fileName:file.name, count:0 };
      alert('Billing file ma USERNAME column vetiena.');
      return;
    }
    const userKey = findKey(json[0], ['username']);
    if(!userKey){
      alert('Billing file ma USERNAME column vetiena.');
      return;
    }
    const amountKey = findKey(json[0], ['paid amount','amount paid','recharge amount','invoice amount','amount']);
    BILLING_PAID = new Set();
    BILLING_AMOUNT = {};
    json.forEach(r=>{
      const u = normUser(r[userKey]);
      if(!u) return;
      BILLING_PAID.add(u);
      if(amountKey){
        const amt = parseFloat(String(r[amountKey]).replace(/[^0-9.\-]/g,''));
        if(!isNaN(amt)) BILLING_AMOUNT[u] = amt;
      }
    });
    BILLING_META = { fileName:file.name, count:BILLING_PAID.size };
    render();
    renderSummary();
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
});

document.getElementById('exportAllBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  exportCSV(filteredRows(), 'expired_users_all.csv');
});

document.getElementById('uptoDate').addEventListener('change', render);
document.getElementById('oltFilter').addEventListener('change', ()=>{
  render();
  renderHighRisk();
  renderSummary();
});

function setDefaultUptoDate(){
  const today = new Date();
  const iso = today.toISOString().slice(0,10);
  document.getElementById('uptoDate').value = iso;
}

// ---------- load embedded default dataset ----------
function loadEmbedded(){
  const embedded = window.__EMBEDDED_DATA__ || [];
  const rows = embedded.map(r=>({
    Username: r.Username, 'Expiry Date': r['Expiry Date'], 'Transaction Date': r['Transaction Date'],
    Revenue: r.Revenue, 'Plan Name': r.PlanName, Bandwidth: r.Bandwidth
  }));
  RAW = normalizeDataset(rows);
  document.getElementById('dataMeta').textContent = `Default dataset loaded · ${RAW.length} records (aaja samma expire filter le 'Not Renewed' user haru dekhauxa)`;
  setDefaultUptoDate();
  render();
  fbLoadAndMerge();
}
loadEmbedded();
renderHighRisk();

// ---------- Staff login flow ----------
function doLogin(){
  const idRaw = document.getElementById('loginUser').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const pass = document.getElementById('loginPass').value;
  const rec = STAFF_CREDENTIALS[idRaw];
  const errEl = document.getElementById('loginErr');
  if(!rec || rec.pass !== pass){
    errEl.textContent = 'Galat Staff ID ya Password.';
    return;
  }
  errEl.textContent = '';
  CURRENT_STAFF = { id: idRaw, name: rec.name, role: rec.role };
  SESSION_START = Date.now();
  document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'none';
  const badge = document.getElementById('staffBadge');
  badge.style.display = 'inline-flex';
  badge.innerHTML = `👤 ${CURRENT_STAFF.name} <button id="logoutBtn">Logout</button>`;
  document.getElementById('logoutBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    stopHeartbeat();
    CURRENT_STAFF = null;
    document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display = 'flex';
    document.getElementById('loginUser').value='';
    document.getElementById('loginPass').value='';
    badge.style.display = 'none';
  });
  startHeartbeat();
  fbLogSession('login');
  renderSummary();
}
document.getElementById('loginBtn').addEventListener('click', doLogin);
document.getElementById('loginPass').addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{ if(e.key==='Enter') doLogin(); });
document.getElementById('loginUser').addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{ if(e.key==='Enter') doLogin(); });

// ---------- Time / effort tracking ----------
let HEARTBEAT_TIMER = null;
function todayStr(){ return new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); }
function startHeartbeat(){
  if(HEARTBEAT_TIMER) return;
  HEARTBEAT_TIMER = setInterval(()=>{
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF || !fbdb) return;
    if(document.visibilityState !== 'visible') return; // only count active/foreground time
    const path = `expiryTracker/staffTime/${todayStr()}/${CURRENT_STAFF.id}`;
    fbdb.ref(path).transaction(cur=>{
      const c = cur || { minutes:0, name: CURRENT_STAFF.name };
      c.minutes = (c.minutes||0) + 1;
      c.name = CURRENT_STAFF.name;
      c.lastActive = Date.now();
      return c;
    });
  }, 60000); // 1 heartbeat per minute of active foreground time
}
function stopHeartbeat(){
  if(HEARTBEAT_TIMER){ clearInterval(HEARTBEAT_TIMER); HEARTBEAT_TIMER = null; }
  fbLogSession('logout');
}
function fbLogSession(kind){
  if(!fbdb || !CURRENT_STAFF) return;
  try{
    fbdb.ref('expiryTracker/sessionLog').push({
      staff: CURRENT_STAFF.id, name: CURRENT_STAFF.name, kind, ts: Date.now()
    });
  }catch(err){ console.error('session log failed', err); }
}
window.addEventListener('beforeunload', ()=>{ if(CURRENT_STAFF) fbLogSession('close'); });

// ---------- High-risk panel ----------
function renderHighRisk(){
  const oltSel = document.getElementById('oltFilter') ? document.getElementById('oltFilter').value : 'ALL';
  const data = (window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__ || []).filter(u=> oltSel==='ALL' || u.OLT===oltSel);
  const sorted = [...data].sort((a,b)=> parseFloat(b.RiskScore||0) - parseFloat(a.RiskScore||0));
  document.getElementById('highRiskSub').textContent = `${sorted.length} habitual/high-risk users (curated list) — Filter: ${oltSel}`;
  const tbody = document.getElementById('highRiskBody');
  function rowHtml(u){
    const rec = HR_FOLLOWUPS[normUser(u.Username)] || {};
    const scoreClass = parseFloat(u.RiskScore||0) >= 90 ? 'risk-hi' : 'risk-med';
    return `
      <tr>
        <td>${u.Username}</td>
        <td>${u.OLT}</td>
        <td class="${scoreClass}">${u.RiskScore}</td>
        <td>${u.Type||''}</td>
        <td>${u.Status||''}</td>
        <td>${u.ForeRevenue||''}</td>
        <td><button class="followBtn hrFollowBtn ${rec.followUp?'done':''}" data-uname="${u.Username}">${rec.followUp ? '✓ Followed up' : 'Mark follow-up'}</button></td>
        <td>${rec.followUp ? remarksSelectHtml(u.Username, rec.remarks) : ''}</td>
      </tr>`;
  }
  tbody.innerHTML = sorted.map(rowHtml).join('');
}
// Event delegation attached ONCE (tbody innerHTML is replaced on every render, but the
// tbody element itself persists, so a single delegated listener is enough — avoids
// duplicate-listener bugs from re-attaching inside renderHighRisk()).
document.getElementById('highRiskBody').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('hrFollowBtn')){
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); return; }
    const uname = e.target.dataset.uname;
    const key = normUser(uname);
    const rec = HR_FOLLOWUPS[key] || { username: uname, followUp:false, remarks:'' };
    rec.followUp = !rec.followUp;
    if(!rec.followUp){ rec.remarks=''; rec.followedUpBy=null; rec.followedUpByName=null; }
    else { rec.followedUpBy = CURRENT_STAFF.id; rec.followedUpByName = CURRENT_STAFF.name; }
    HR_FOLLOWUPS[key] = rec;
    fbSaveFollowup(uname, rec.followUp, rec.remarks, 'highrisk');
    renderHighRisk();
    renderSummary();
  }
});
document.getElementById('highRiskBody').addEventListener('change', (e)=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('remarksInput')){
    if(!CURRENT_STAFF){ alert('Pahile login garnus.'); e.target.value=''; return; }
    const uname = e.target.dataset.uname;
    const key = normUser(uname);
    const rec = HR_FOLLOWUPS[key] || { username: uname, followUp:true, remarks:'' };
    rec.remarks = e.target.value;
    rec.followedUpBy = CURRENT_STAFF.id; rec.followedUpByName = CURRENT_STAFF.name;
    HR_FOLLOWUPS[key] = rec;
    fbSaveFollowup(uname, rec.followUp, rec.remarks, 'highrisk');
    renderSummary();
  }
});

function remarksSelectHtml(uname, currentVal){
  const opts = REMARK_OPTIONS.map(o=>`<option value="${o}" ${o===currentVal?'selected':''}>${o}</option>`).join('');
  return `<select class="remarksInput" data-uname="${uname}"><option value="" ${!currentVal?'selected':''}>-- Select --</option>${opts}</select>`;
}

// ---------- Staff-wise Summary Report ----------
function renderSummary(){
  const wrap = document.getElementById('summaryPanel');
  if(!wrap) return;
  const oltSel = document.getElementById('oltFilter') ? document.getElementById('oltFilter').value : 'ALL';
  const hrOltByUser = {}; // normalized username -> OLT, from curated high-risk list
  (window.__HIGH_RISK_DATA__ || []).forEach(u=>{ hrOltByUser[normUser(u.Username)] = u.OLT; });
  const staffStats = {}; // id -> {name, followUps, categories:{}, collected:0}
  function ensure(id, name){
    if(!staffStats[id]) staffStats[id] = { name: name||id, followUps:0, categories:{}, collected:0 };
    return staffStats[id];
  }
  // from main dataset
  RAW.forEach(r=>{
    if(oltSel!=='ALL' && r.olt!==oltSel) return;
    if(r.followUp && r.followedUpBy){
      const s = ensure(r.followedUpBy, r.followedUpByName);
      s.followUps++;
      const cat = r.remarks || 'Uncategorized';
      s.categories[cat] = (s.categories[cat]||0) + 1;
      if(BILLING_PAID.has(normUser(r.username))){
        s.collected += (BILLING_AMOUNT[normUser(r.username)] != null ? BILLING_AMOUNT[normUser(r.username)] : r.revenue);
      }
    }
  });
  // from high-risk dataset
  Object.values(HR_FOLLOWUPS).forEach(rec=>{
    if(oltSel!=='ALL' && hrOltByUser[normUser(rec.username)]!==oltSel) return;
    if(rec.followUp && rec.followedUpBy){
      const s = ensure(rec.followedUpBy, rec.followedUpByName);
      s.followUps++;
      const cat = rec.remarks || 'Uncategorized';
      s.categories[cat] = (s.categories[cat]||0) + 1;
      if(BILLING_PAID.has(normUser(rec.username))){
        s.collected += (BILLING_AMOUNT[normUser(rec.username)] || 0);
      }
    }
  });
  // effort hours from Firebase cache
  Object.keys(STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL).forEach(id=>{
    ensure(id, STAFF_CREDENTIALS[id] ? STAFF_CREDENTIALS[id].name : id);
  });

  const ids = Object.keys(staffStats);
  if(!ids.length){
    wrap.innerHTML = `<div class="note">Halsam kunai staff le follow-up gareko chaina. Login garera follow-up marking start garnus.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  let html = `<div class="scrollx"><table><thead><tr>
    <th>Staff</th><th>Total Follow-ups</th><th>Category breakdown</th><th>Effort (hrs, aaja)</th><th>Amount Collected (NPR)</th>
  </tr></thead><tbody>`;
  ids.forEach(id=>{
    const s = staffStats[id];
    const minutes = STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL[id] || 0;
    const hrs = (minutes/60).toFixed(1);
    const cats = Object.entries(s.categories).map(([k,v])=>`<span class="cat-pill">${k}: ${v}</span>`).join(' ') || '—';
    html += `<tr>
      <td><b>${s.name}</b></td>
      <td>${fmtNum(s.followUps)}</td>
      <td>${cats}</td>
      <td>${hrs} hr</td>
      <td>${fmtNum(s.collected)}</td>
    </tr>`;
  });
  html += '</tbody></table></div>';
  wrap.innerHTML = html;
}

function fbLoadStaffTime(){
  if(!fbdb) return;
  fbdb.ref(`expiryTracker/staffTime/${todayStr()}`).on('value', snap=>{
    const data = snap.val() || {};
    STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL = {};
    Object.keys(data).forEach(id=>{ STAFF_MINUTES_TODAY_ALL[id] = data[id].minutes || 0; });
    renderSummary();
  });
}
fbLoadStaffTime();
</script>
</body>
</html>
